The 2018-19 workbook does not use the shared map's reading of codes `CSG` and `ACD`. `config/demographic_mappings.yaml` sends `CSG` to Students with Disabilities and lists `ACD` in `filter_out`. In this workbook `DGROUP` says `CSG` is Consolidated Student Group and `ACD` is `Studw/disab.`. State `GT_CNT` is 14,874 on the consolidated row and 3,136 on `Studw/disab.`. The 2020 Students with Disabilities state total is 3,357, which is the disability series, not 14,874. The shared map stored the 14,874 as Students with Disabilities and dropped the disability row: 30,993 numeric cells and 1,484 stars. This module copies `DGROUP` into the demographic field for `GIFTED_AND_TALENTED_18-19.xlsx` only, before the shared mapper runs. `Studw/disab.` is already a year-2019 mapping to Students with Disabilities. Consolidated Student Group is already a standard label, so it stays. The other `DGROUP` labels (`All Students`, `EL`, `Free/Reduced`, `Hispanic`, `Military Connected`, and the race and gender labels) land on the same groups the codes do. `demographic_mappings.yaml` is not edited. A file that still carries the code `CSG` keeps the shared map. Issue #76 (https://github.com/jscotthorn/ky-education-kpi-pipeline/issues/76) asks the other modules to check their 2018-19 workbooks for the same swap.

The base parser, on the files registered before this change, wrote 351,701 rows and dropped every star. That file is 79,178,133 bytes. Joined on year, school id, student group, and metric, 339,726 of those rows match this output with 0 value differences. The other 11,975 are the 2019 Students with Disabilities rows. They are the consolidated-group cells, and they match Consolidated Student Group with 0 value differences. The disability count is new: 32,477 rows, state all-grades 3,136. By year the base file is 164,485 / 31,580 / 31,644 / 32,844 / 45,445 / 45,703 for 2019 and 2021-2025. 2020 and 2026 were not in it. The added published numbers, besides that disability count, are the category counts, `TOTAL COUNT` and `KINDERGARTEN COUNT` on the 2021-2023 files (the old map had `KINDERGARTEN` and `K`, not `KINDERGARTEN COUNT`, and it did not map `TOTAL COUNT`), the 2020 file, the KYRC26 file, and the category-only zeros. 2021 adds 9,497 all-grades totals and 1,210 kindergarten counts (10,707). 2022 adds 9,610 all-grades totals, after the duplicate 3 is dropped, and 1,744 kindergarten counts (11,354). 2023 adds 9,808 and 1,838 (11,646). 2024 adds no new grade numbers. 2025 adds the 220 zeros and no other grade numbers.

On pandas 3.0.6 the transform takes 352 seconds and peaks at 992 MB resident. On pandas 2.3.3 it takes 305 seconds and peaks at 1,192 MB. The two versions write the same 4,375,413 rows apart from `last_updated` (md5 of every other column `ca99a66927401687c5456308cf4761dd`). The grade-file schools are read once up front (12,801 year-and-school keys) and the 4,533 category totals are left out while the file is written. The output is not read back. The previous post-write sort reread the CSV, about 363 seconds and 4.26 GB, and the full run peaked at 3.97 GB on one pandas and 4.42 GB on the other. The notes/69 target is under 4 GB. The base parser peaked at 312 MB. This output is 1,019,413,517 bytes.

2022 repeats Covington Independent school code `134` once per demographic (18 keys, 36 rows). Both copies are `---District Total---`. All Students totals are 3 and 105. The 3-row's other measure cells are stars. The school sum of numeric All Students cells is 100, with 4 starred schools. A number beats a star, and the larger number wins, so 105 is kept. John G. Carlisle Elementary `134110` is a separate row whose total is also 3. It is not the dropped copy. Other years have no repeated school-and-demographic key. `gifted_and_talented_2023.csv` has 18 rows with school year `20022023`, the Breathitt County district total, school code `061`, All Students 239. The schools under `20222023` sum to 239. `extract_year` keeps the last four digits, so the row is year 2023. It is the only Breathitt district total. It is kept.

`KYRC23_EDOP_Gifted_Participation_by_Grade_Level.csv` and the category twin return 200 on datasets and on UAT and 404 on the historical host. The two containers are byte-identical (grade 2,028,075 bytes, md5 `0b0c3812522916bafb972156bf3f91e1`; category 3,167,258 bytes, md5 `63934711ed302972daab3ba56811d327`). School year is `20222023` on every row. State All Grades is 87,613. On All Students, comparing the codes as published, 1,265 are on both files, 392 are only on `gifted_and_talented_2023.csv`, and 174 are only on the KYRC23 file. Those 174 are the six-digit district totals (`001000`) against the historical three-digit district totals (`001`). After that normalization the only-on-KYRC23 count is 0. The 1,193 pairs that are both numbers have 0 differences. The KYRC23 grade file has no preschool and no consolidated student group, and its district codes are six digits. The historical file has those columns and three-digit district codes. Loading both would write the shared 2023 schools twice. `accepts_data_file` skips the two KYRC23 names. They are not registered. The guessed OAA names (`Gifted_Talented_2024.CSV`, `Gifted_Talented_2025.CSV`, `Gifted_Talented_2026.CSV`, and the participation twins) are 404 and were never registered, so they are not skipped. `GIFTED_AND_TALENTED_17-18.xlsx` returns 200 (4,682,023 bytes, md5 `a85273bb1d94401a62dfab044c35e26a`, sheets `COVER SHEET` and `DATA`, school year `20172018`, 26,586 DATA rows). It is earlier than this series and is not registered.

`gifted_and_talented_2020.csv` returns 200 on the historical host and 404 on datasets, UAT, and the OAA host. It is 5,463,357 bytes, md5 `849d73f3d857024c6d5ce4f00efa17e0`, school year `20192020` only, 29,718 rows. State total 96,290. It is registered. Every other registered URL returned 200 on 18 October 2026.

pandas is unpinned. `dtype=str` is StringDtype on pandas 3. The comma strip treats StringDtype the same as object. It does not change a previously published row. It is what keeps `90,464` under an Int64 cast. `config/mappings.yaml` has no dtype cast for this source. `bool(pd.NA)` is not used. pandas 2.3.3 and pandas 3.0.6 write the same 4,375,413 rows apart from `last_updated`. `BaseETL` was not edited. The pandas 3.0.6 run takes 352 seconds and peaks at 992 MB.

Issue #42 (https://github.com/jscotthorn/ky-education-kpi-pipeline/issues/42): `scripts/generate_report_drafts.py` selects state rows with `district == 'State'`. These metrics are not in that script. State district is `State` for 2019-2023 and `All Districts` for 2024-2026. School name `All Schools` is stored as `---District Total---`. `---State Total---` is left as written. The 2019-2023 state school code stays `999`. KYRC24 through KYRC26 use `999000`. Do not pad.

These metric names are emitted only by `etl/gifted_talented.py`. `kpi_master` concatenates module files without deduplicating metric names.

Fixtures are verbatim rows in `tests/fixtures/gifted_talented/`. `tests/test_fixture_sizes.py` passes. Each fixture is under 50 KB.

Lessons for later agents:

1. HEAD the registered URLs before assuming a temporary file went 404. Every registered URL returned 200 on 18 October 2026. The guessed `Gifted_Talented_*.CSV` names are 404 and were never registered. Do not add a skip for a filename this directory has never used. `KYRC23_EDOP_Gifted_Participation_by_Grade_Level.csv` and the category twin return 200 on datasets and 404 on the historical host. School year is `20222023`. State All Grades is 87,613, the same cell as `gifted_and_talented_2023.csv`. Skip those two names. Do not register them. `gifted_and_talented_2020.csv` is school year `20192020` and is registered. `GIFTED_AND_TALENTED_17-18.xlsx` returns 200 and is school year `20172018`. It is earlier than the series. Do not register it.
2. The grade total is an unduplicated count of gifted students. A category count is an identification, and a student may be in more than one category. Keep the category on `gifted_category_count_{slug}`. The category file's All Students cell is the unduplicated total. Store it as `gifted_participation_count_all_grades` and keep the grade-file row when both exist. The 2018-19 coversheet says `AcademicArts` is art. The state count is the later language-arts series. Do not match the column with a search for `ART`. `advanced_coursework` skips course type `DC` and the text `Dual Credit`. Issue #74 (a) is fixed in that module. Do not load dual credit there. Questions (b) and (c) stay open for Scott.
3. KYRC26 has a state row `999000`, district `All Districts`, school name `All Schools` stored as `---District Total---`, year `2026`. The state total is 90,464. Fayette County `165000` is 10,937. Bryan Station High `165170` is 238. Districts equal the state in 2026. The 2019 and 2020 district sums sit about 1,000 above the state. That gap is the same shape in those two years and is not a 2026 break. `GREC` becomes `GLEC`. `GRREC` stays `GRREC`.
4. pandas is unpinned. `dtype=str` is StringDtype on pandas 3. The comma strip treats StringDtype the same as object. It does not change a previously published row. It is what keeps `90,464` under an Int64 cast. pandas 2.3.3 and pandas 3.0.6 write the same rows apart from `last_updated`. `BaseETL` was not edited. `test_standardize_missing_values` was a journal 105 failure and now passes. Drop category All Students totals before writing. Do not reread the output to sort it. The pandas 3.0.6 transform peaks at 992 MB. pandas 2.3.3 peaks at 1,192 MB.
5. These files have no Suppressed column. A `*` on a measure cell is stored as value NA with suppressed Y. A blank is not a star and is not stored. Record the star in `standardize_missing_values` before the cell becomes NA. A row with no `_star_` column still treats `*` as a star and a blank as a skip. A published 0 is kept where the file publishes one: the 4,541 grade-14 zeros in 2019, and the 220 and 237 category-only school totals in 2025 and 2026. The 2020-2023 files publish no 0 and no blank. The smallest number is 3, so a Y in those years is not evidence the group exists. Preschool, grade 14, and Consolidated Student Group are starred on every 2020-2023 row. A blank preschool cell is not a zero. Grade 14 is absent only in 2026. The KYRC25 state cell is a star and is stored.
6. Compare the code sets, then the counts. Grade-file schools are 1,447 − 46 + 23 = 1,424. Category schools are 1,667 − 16 + 10 = 1,661. Category source rows are 13 times the school count. KPI rows are not source rows times the grade columns, because a blank grade cell is omitted and a star is stored. KYRC26 writes 89,434. The cross-file drop is 4,533 keys and 0 value disagreements. Drop the category All Students row before writing. Do not reread the output CSV to sort it.
7. Published rows against the base code: 339,726 match on the same student group, and the 11,975 base 2019 Students with Disabilities rows match Consolidated Student Group. 2024 grade numbers are unchanged. 2019's disability count is `Studw/disab.`, state 3,136, and it was not in the base file. 2021-2023 gain `TOTAL COUNT` and `KINDERGARTEN COUNT`. 2020 and 2026 are new. The total is 4,375,413.
8. State district is `All Districts` for 2024-2026 and `State` for 2019-2023. School name `All Schools` is stored as `---District Total---`. `---State Total---` is left as written. The 2019-2023 state school code stays `999`. Do not pad. Issue #42 does not list these metrics. The 2026 state total 90,464 is the next point after 90,490.
9. No shared parser or dependency changes. The historical KYRC24 extract stays registered. Its numbers match the datasets copy where both publish a number. The datasets copy drops Preschool, turns historical stars into blanks, and uses the long category shape. Those filled blanks are not loaded. KYRC25 has no value difference between the historical file, datasets, and UAT.
10. 2022 repeats Covington Independent `134` for every demographic. Keep the larger All Students total, 105 rather than 3. A number beats a star. Other years do not repeat a school-and-demographic key. The Breathitt `20022023` district total is year 2023 and is the only Breathitt district total. Do not skip it.

Lessons for later agents:

1. HEAD the registered URLs before assuming a file is missing. The historical host returns 403 without a browser User-Agent and 200 with one. Every registered URL returned 200 on 18 October 2026. `Advanced_Coursework_2026.CSV` is 404 and was never registered. Do not add a skip for it. The two KYRC23 EDOP names return 200 and are skipped by exact filename. `KYRC23_OVW_Advanced_Coursework.csv` is registered. KYRC24 and KYRC25 overview are skipped by exact filename.
2. Course type `DC` is dual credit. The text `Dual Credit` is also dual credit. Skip both. Do not emit `dual_credit_*` here. Course type `ALL` is the only `advanced_*` series, and it includes dual-credit students. Course type `CAI` is Cambridge. An unrecognized type is skipped. Do not fold it into `advanced`. The 2020-2022 DC counts are stored nowhere until issue #74 (b).
3. Participation, courses offered, and the CRDC overview are three measures. Courses offered do not use `{prefix}_course_enrollment`. A subject total uses the subject. A course uses the state course code and the course name, because 2023 reuses codes. `num_{prefix}_courses_offered` counts course rows, not the `All Enrollments` total. `advanced_offered_*` exists only for 2025 and 2026.
4. Do not derive a qualifying rate for course type `ALL`. The 2023 state quotient is 49,618 / 26,451. AP and Cambridge rates are qualifying exams divided by students tested. Do not derive `ib_qualifying_score_rate` for 2026. No AP row in 2020-2026 has qualifying scores above students tested.
5. A `*` is stored as value NA with suppressed Y. Record the star before the cell becomes NA. A blank is not a star. The 2020-2026 participation files publish no 0 and no blank, so a Y is not evidence the group exists. 2019 publishes 0 and `*`. A Y in 2019 is not a published 0. A published overview 0 is kept. KYRC24 writes 0 where KYRC25 is blank. That edition is not loaded.
6. The 2018-19 sheet's disability group is the label on code `ACD`, `Students with Disability with IEP`. Code `CSG` is Consolidated Student Group, and every measure cell is blank. Read `DISAGGGROUP`. Do not read `DEMO_ABBREV` through the shared map.
7. KYRC26 state AP enrollment 37,212 is the next point after 35,687. Districts remain slightly above the state. The same-school median ratio stays about 1.03. IB tested 42 is not the next point after 234. The offered file the same year is 240 tested and 172 qualifying. Do not store the derived participation rate. Issue #78 holds the count. Cambridge stays continuous.
8. State district is `All Districts` for 2024-2026 and `State` for 2019-2023. School name `All Schools` is stored as `---District Total---`. `---State Total---` is left as written. The 2019 courses-offered state code `000` is stored as `999`. Do not pad other codes. Issue #42 does not list these metrics.
9. No shared parser or dependency changes. pandas is unpinned. The comma strip is local. The pandas 3.0.6 transform peaks at 221,684 KB in 61.6 seconds. pandas 2.3.3 peaks at 248,300 KB in 59.6 seconds. Both write 648,001 rows. Do not reread the output to post-process it.
10. Store each CRDC overview collection once, under the collection year. The 2020-21 collection is KPI year 2021, from KYRC23, because it matches KYRC25 on every shared cell and has 15 more schools. KYRC24 and KYRC25 overview are skipped by exact filename. The 2021-22 collection is KPI year 2022, from the KYRC26 overview file. Overview names do not collide with the 2021 or 2022 participation names, so they stay `{prefix}_participation_count` and `{prefix}_participation_rate`. Course type `ALL` includes dual-credit students: the 2023 state enrollment 68,238 sits between AP+IB+Cambridge 34,777 and that sum plus DC 80,650. `advanced_offered_*` exists only for 2025 and 2026. The 2020-2022 DC counts are stored nowhere until issue #74 (b). Do not derive the 2026 IB qualifying rate. The 2019 courses-offered state code `000` is stored as `999`.

KDE's 2018-19 coversheet defines this extract as specialized courses: CTE, HPE, VPA, and WL. `Total_Students` is students enrolled in the course. The DATA sheet has no `DEMO` and no `DGROUP` column, so issue #76's abbreviation swap does not apply. The shared demographic yaml was not edited. Every KPI row is `student_group` `All Students`.

There is no `School Code` `999000` and no `All Schools` row in any registered year. The journal 104 fixture line that asked for state `999000` does not match the file. Bryan Station High School `165170` is the school check. Adair County is the district on Adair County High School `001010`; the file does not publish a district total, so there is nothing to compare with a sum of schools. `co_op_code` `901` on the Fayette rows is the CKEC cooperative code. The school code stays `165170`. KYRC26 replaces `GREC` with `GLEC` (Echo Trail Middle `275255`, co-op code `909`). Nothing in this pipeline groups on the co-op name.

Scott's decision on issue #82 collapses the per-course 1/NA rows. The output is one row per school, year, and student group. `courses_offered_count` is the number of course rows. A starred total counts. A published 0 would count. No registered file has a 0 total. A blank total is not stored and is not counted. No registered file has a blank total. `courses_offered_suppressed_count` is how many of the counted rows have a `*` total, and it is 0 when there are none. Every one of these metrics is stored with `suppressed` N. Grade cells are not added into the count. A `*` on a grade cell does not remove a published total. In 2020-2026 the smallest published total is 3, so a starred total in those years can also mean 0-2 or not applicable. 2019 publishes totals down to 1 and has no stars (minimum 1, maximum 1,655), so every 2019 suppressed count is 0.

The category is KDE's own column. The 2018-19 coversheet and the 2019 `COURSECATEGORY` column use `CTE`, `HPE`, `VPA`, and `WL`. Files from 2020 through 2026 spell health and physical education `HEPE`. `HPE` and `HEPE` both map to `courses_offered_count_hpe`. A blank or unrecognized label would be `courses_offered_count_other`. No registered year has one, so `_other` is 0. The state course code is not used: visual arts and health/PE both use prefix 70, and 2022 has 4,553 five-digit codes that the column already labels. The four named categories plus `_other` sum to `courses_offered_count`.

`algebra_1_grade_8_count` and `has_algebra_1_access` are still emitted per course, and only when the course name contains `Algebra I` or `Algebra 1`. No registered file has such a name, in any year. Those two metrics are not in the collapsed output. They are issue #83 and were not changed.

The first pass of this PR stored one row per course, value 1 or NA/Y. That is the "before" column. It had 247,162 rows and 10,757 distinct keys. The "after" column is the collapsed KPI rows: seven metrics times the number of schools. The 2019, 2021, and 2023 values change from 1 to the school counts. That is the decision on #82.

KYRC files use a blank grade cell where the 2020-2023 historical files use `*`. A blank grade next to a published total is not a suppressed course. The locked blank-grade example is Adair County High School `001010`, Accounting and Finance Foundations `060122`: KYRC26 total 37, grade 8 blank. The school has 111 courses and 10 starred totals, so `courses_offered_count` is 111 and `courses_offered_suppressed_count` is 10, both suppressed N. The KYRC25 twin of that course is total 35. NCES id `210003000000` in KYRC25 and `210003000001` in KYRC26. About 96% of KYRC25 NCES ids are rounded to a trailing zero. That is a known cross-module issue. This module stores the published id and does not repair it.

The locked star is the same school, Advanced Studio Production `480912`, total `*`. It adds 1 to the course count and 1 to the suppressed count. Structures & Design `010211`, total 25: the school count is 2 and the suppressed count is 1 on the two-row fixture. The 2021 anchor is Allied Health Core Skills `170501`, total 59, with the same starred companion.

The grade cells are not summed into a replacement total. No comma in the 2019-2023 historical totals. KYRC24 has 3, KYRC25 has 3, KYRC26 has 7. The comma strip is dtype-agnostic. `config/mappings.yaml` has no Int64 cast for this source. pandas is unpinned. `BaseETL.standardize_missing_values` was not edited.

The transform, without reading the output back, peaks at 232,060 KB / 26.9 s on pandas 3.0.6 and 243,676 KB / 27.7 s on pandas 2.3.3. Both write 75,299 rows.

Lessons for the next agent. Do not require a `999000` row. Map `All Students` only if that column is the enrollment total. A blank grade is not a blank row. A starred total counts toward `courses_offered_count` and `courses_offered_suppressed_count`. A blank total does not. Take the category from KDE's column. `HPE` and `HEPE` are both health and PE. Do not classify from the course-code prefix. Do not repair the rounded KYRC25 NCES ids. Algebra metrics stay per course and are issue #83. The 32 starred-total-with-grades rows are inside the school counts; do not sum their grade cells.

### `cte_pathways` — S

| | |
| --- | --- |
| KYRC26 files | `KYRC26_CTE_Career_Pathways.csv` |
| Header changes | None |
| Fixtures / assertions | State `999000` row, year `2026` |
| Gotchas | District `606` is new versus KYRC25. Not a header change |

Result, checked 18 October 2026 against the public `datasets` container for KYRC25 and KYRC26 and the historical host for the other registered files. `include_kyrc26 = True` is the parser change. `Pathways Available` is the pathway name in every year, so the old parser emitted `num_cte_pathways` zero times and wrote one KPI row per pathway. Those rows shared a key. The output is now one row per school, year, and student group. `etl/kyrc.py`, `BaseETL`, and the demographic config were not edited. pandas is unpinned. Re-HEAD `KYRC26_CTE_Career_Pathways.csv` after 26 October 2026. The datasets object checked here is 889,814 bytes, Last-Modified `Thu, 15 Oct 2026 10:00:09 GMT`. The UAT object is the same length, Last-Modified `Sat, 10 Oct 2026 06:00:07 GMT`.

KDE's glossary defines active enrollment as the number of students enrolled during the school year in at least one valid course in that pathway. A concentrator is a 12th-grade student with at least two and fewer than four credits in that pathway. A completer is a 12th-grade student with four or more credits in that pathway. Those are different groups. A student in two pathways is on two rows, so the stored sums count that student twice. State and district figures are KDE's own pathway rows. This module does not replace a state row with the sum of districts or a district row with the sum of schools.

`num_cte_pathways` is the number of pathway names. A starred count still counts. A published 0 counts. A row whose enrollment, concentrator, and completer cells are all blank is not offered and is not counted. No registered file has that blank row. The count is stored with `suppressed` N. `cte_pathway_enrollment`, `cte_concentrator_count`, and `cte_pathway_completer_count` are the sums. If any component is `*`, the total is value NA with `suppressed` Y. A partial sum is not stored. `cte_pathway_completion_rate` is completers divided by concentrators, rounded to one decimal, and only when both totals are published and concentrators are above 0. When either total is suppressed, the rate is NA with `suppressed` Y. KDE does not publish that ratio.

In 2020-2026 the smallest published count is 3 and there is no blank, so a star can also mean 0-2 or not applicable. 2019 publishes 0 and has no stars. Eighteen of the 167 state pathways in 2019 are enrollment 0 and completers 0. Those zeros are kept.

2020-2023 list some pathways twice, under two program areas, with the same three counts: 79, 88, 93, and 105 pairs. None of the pairs disagree. The pathway is counted once. The old parser added both copies. For the entities whose new total is published, 2024 and 2025 match the sum of the old per-pathway rows exactly (enrollment 176 / 204, concentrators 194 / 239, completers 36 / 44). 2021 and 2023 match after the duplicate copy is removed: all 5 enrollment gaps in 2021 and all 10 in 2023 are that second copy. No registered pair disagrees. A disagreement would suppress that measure rather than add the two numbers.

The old output, before this change, was 65,475 rows: 2021 was 15,360, 2023 was 16,137, 2024 was 16,777, and 2025 was 17,201. None were suppressed. 58,330 keys were duplicated. `num_cte_pathways` was 0. The new counts are below. 2020, 2022, and 2019 were not loaded before.

| Year | Source rows | Before (per-pathway KPI rows) | After (KPI rows) | Entities |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| 2019 | 6,356 | not loaded | 1,392 | 464 |
| 2020 | 5,674 | not loaded | 2,335 | 467 |
| 2021 | 5,589 | 15,360 | 2,325 | 465 |
| 2022 | 5,814 | not loaded | 2,350 | 470 |
| 2023 | 6,034 | 16,137 | 2,350 | 470 |
| 2024 | 5,564 | 16,777 | 2,350 | 470 |
| 2025 | 5,594 | 17,201 | 2,380 | 476 |
| 2026 | 5,662 | not loaded | 2,410 | 482 |

The full run writes 17,892 rows. 2019 has three metrics because there is no concentrator column and no rate. The other years have five. Suppressed KPI rows are 0 / 1,546 / 1,535 / 1,607 / 1,614 / 1,438 / 1,373 / 1,465 for 2019-2026. In 2026, 64.5% of enrollment totals, 54.8% of concentrator totals, and 92.3% of completer totals are suppressed because at least one pathway cell is `*`. The state enrollment total is suppressed in every year from 2020 through 2026 for that reason. The published state Agribusiness Systems row in KYRC26 is enrollment `2,546`, concentrators `2,002`, completers 352. Those cells are inside the suppressed state totals. They are not stored as their own rows.

State pathway counts, after the duplicate program-area listing is removed: 167, 130, 121, 119, 120, 107, 107, 113 for 2019-2026. School pathway-count medians stay at 10 or 11. Among schools whose enrollment total is fully published, the median is 522 in 2025 and 502 in 2026. Program-area labels are renamed in KYRC26 (`Agriculture` becomes `Agricultural Education`, and the other areas move with them). The program area is not a metric.

Locked KYRC26 values. State `999000`, district `All Districts`, year `2026`: `num_cte_pathways` 113, suppressed N. Enrollment, concentrators, completers, and the rate are suppressed Y (9, 6, and 15 starred pathway cells). District `606000`, Model Laboratory Schools at EKU, is new versus KYRC25: 2 pathways, enrollment 51 (Management and Entrepreneurship 14 plus Culinary and Food Services 37), concentrators and completers suppressed Y. Model High School `606460` publishes the same two pathways and the same 51. Caldwell County High School `081010`: 7 pathways, enrollment 556, concentrators 413, completers 143, rate 34.6, all suppressed N. Bellevue High School `032010`: enrollment suppressed Y because Accounting is `*`, concentrators 41. Bryan Station High School `165170`: 14 pathways, enrollment suppressed Y. Adair County High School `001010` matches its district total and both are suppressed. 2019 state `999`, district `State`: 167 pathways, enrollment 249,895, completers 22,289, no concentrator metric. There is no DEMO or DGROUP column, so issue #76 does not apply to this workbook.

`KYRC23_CTE_Career_Pathways.csv` returns 200 on datasets and UAT (889,993 bytes, school year `20222023`) and 404 on the historical host. Padding a 3-digit historical district code with `000` makes the pathway keys match `career_pathways_2023.csv` (5,929 keys, nothing on only one side). Enrollment and concentrator cells match after the comma strip. 29 completer cells are `*` in the historical file and a number in KYRC23. 118 program-area labels differ. `accepts_data_file` skips the KYRC23 name. It is not registered. Twelve historical 2023 rows for district `061` say school year `20022023`. The last four digits are 2023, and those pathways are not also on a `20222023` row, so they stay in 2023.

`career_pathways_2020.csv` (840,684 bytes, school year `20192020`) and `career_pathways_2022.csv` (879,983 bytes, school year `20212022`) return 200 on the historical host and are registered. `CAREER_PATHWAYS_18-19.xlsx` returns 200. The pathway column is a name, as it is in every later file. Preparatory students are not mapped to concentrators. `CAREER_PATHWAYS_17-18.xlsx` returns 200 and is earlier than this series. It is not registered.

The historical KYRC25 file is byte-identical to the datasets object (827,367 bytes). The datasets KYRC24 object is 811,160 bytes and the registered historical file is 810,003 bytes. Measure cells match. 290 co-op cells differ. The historical file stays registered. Same filename, so the datasets copy is not a second registration.

Guessed OAA names `Career_Pathways_2024.CSV`, `Career_Pathways_2025.CSV`, and `Career_Pathways_2026.CSV` are 404 on the historical host, both blob containers, and the OAA host. They were never registered, so they are not skipped.

Where a pathway has no stars at the state and no stars at any district, the 2021 state cell equals the sum of the deduped district cells. KYRC25 and KYRC26 do not. KYRC26 enrollment: Automotive Maintenance and Light Repair Technician is 3,960 at the state and 3,964 across districts, and Electrical Technician is 1,010 and 1,011. KYRC26 concentrators: Welder - Entry Level is 4,137 and 4,190, U.S. Army JROTC is 3,398 and 3,421, and Plant Science Systems is 7,333 and 7,353. The districts are higher in each of those cases. The stored row is still the published level. Issue text is in the pull request. Co-op `GREC` is `GLEC` on the KYRC26 rows (130 KPI rows). Nothing in this pipeline groups on the co-op.

No comma in the 2019-2023 historical counts. KYRC files use thousands commas. The comma strip reads the copied source text and does not check `dtype == object`. `config/mappings.yaml` has no Int64 cast for this source. `BaseETL.standardize_missing_values` was not edited.

The transform, without reading the output back, peaks at 113,656 KB / 5.1 s on pandas 3.0.6 and 118,156 KB / 4.6 s on pandas 2.3.3. Both write 17,892 rows.

Lessons for the next agent. `Pathways Available` is a name, not a count, in this file and in the 2018-19 workbook. Do not parse it with `to_numeric`. Aggregate one row per school when KDE publishes one row per pathway, and do not add the same pathway twice when two program areas repeat it. A star suppresses the sum. A blank row is not a pathway. A published 0 is kept. Do not map preparatory students to concentrators. Do not treat a 2018-19 file as having no demographic column until you look: this workbook has no DEMO or DGROUP column, and issue #76 does not apply here. KYRC state rows are district `All Districts`. 2019-2023 state rows in this file are district `State` and school id `999`. Do not pad those ids. `career_readiness_indicators` is the next file. Its headers are not these headers.

## Suggested order

1. `chronic_absenteeism`. Merged. Unchanged headers, state rate 23.5. See the pilot notes above before copying the pattern.
2. `graduation_rates`. In review. State 94.1 and 94.5. The 5-year cohort header was unmapped. See that module's notes before copying the pattern onto the next file.
3. The other opt-in modules above, in the order listed, through `kindergarten_readiness`. `teacher_certification` is in review in PR #71. It stores the published percent. A 0% with a positive teacher count is a real zero. A 0% with a teacher count of 0 is a placeholder and is still stored. A blank percent stays missing. KYRC26 emergency/provisional is a different level from 2020-2025 (state 6,133 / 14.3 after 826 / 1.9). National Board for 2024-2026 continues. Issue #70. Rank I and Rank II are not emitted. A `*` is stored as value NA with suppressed Y. A blank is not stored. 2020-2022 keep the larger National Board copy, which removes 126 / 736 / 1,412 KPI rows. A 0% with a teacher count of 0, or with both counts blank, is still stored. Issue #72. `teacher_turnover` is in review in PR #67. It stores the published percent. A 0% with a positive teacher count is a real zero. A 0% with a teacher count of 0 is a placeholder and is still stored. It does not turn an `N/A` count into 0. KYRC26 district and state rows are a different measure from 2019-2025. Issue #68. Issue #69. Instructional-staff columns are not emitted. `students_taught_by_out_of_field_teachers` is in review in PR #65. It stores the published percent. The group columns are within-group rates. A published 0 is kept. The stored equity gap is signed for 2021-2023 and 2026 and is the absolute value for 2024-25. Issue #59. The 2019-20 file is largely zero-fill and is not loaded. Issue #66 holds that file and the KYRC24 datasets copy. `students_taught_by_ineffective_teachers` is in review in PR #63. It stores the published percent, keeps a published 0, and leaves `N/A` and `#N/A` missing because there is no count column. `teacher_working_conditions` is in review in PR #60. It stores percent favorable, keeps a published 0, and does not turn a literal `N/A` into 0. `novice_teachers` is in review in PR #57. It reads both inexperienced-teacher files from the `novice_teachers` directory. The same student file stays registered under `students_taught_by_inexperienced_teachers` for download only. `student_teacher_ratio` is in review in PR #55. It fixtures a school row and does not rebuild district or state totals. `advanced_coursework` expects 257 overview schools and collected year `20212022`.
4. `kentucky_summative_assessment` after one opt-in has landed.
5. `safe_schools_discipline`, then `safe_schools_events`, then `safe_schools_climate`.
6. `spending_per_student` and `financial_summary` when those KYRC26 names return 200.
7. SAT (`ASMT_CAE`) only if a separate series is in scope. No script for the CRDC rename unless a CRDC KPI is in scope.

No S module needs to become M because of a new header.
